wet_ult_place_C03 = {
  if = {
    limit = {
      check_variable = {
        wet_ult_claim_C03
        >
        0
      }
    }
    clr_country_flag = wet_ult_credit_placed
    if = {
      limit = {
        NOT = {
          has_country_flag = wet_ult_credit_placed
        }
        310 = {
          is_owned_by = H06
          is_fully_controlled_by = H06
          impassable = no
          NOT = {
            OR = {
              fuel_silo
              >
              5
            }
          }
          free_building_slots = {
            building = fuel_silo
            size
            >
            0
            include_locked = no
          }
        }
      }
      310 = {
        add_building_construction = {
          type = fuel_silo
          level = 1
          instant_build = yes
        }
      }
      set_country_flag = wet_ult_credit_placed
    }
    if = {
      limit = {
        has_country_flag = wet_ult_credit_placed
      }
      subtract_from_variable = {
        wet_ult_claim_C03 = 1
      }
    }
    clr_country_flag = wet_ult_credit_placed
  }
}

          infrastructure

          4

    }

wet_ult_start_schism = {
  if = {
    limit = {
      wet_ult_bridgehead = yes
      NOT = {
        has_country_flag = wet_ult_schism_started
      }
      num_of_controlled_states
      >
      1
      any_owned_state = {
        wet_ult_revolt_site = yes
      }
    }
    set_country_flag = wet_ult_schism_started
    if = {
      limit = {
        has_country_flag = wet_ult_loyalty_prepared
      }
      start_civil_war = {
        ideology = neutrality
        ruling_party = neutrality
        size = 0.15
        army_ratio = 0.15
        air_ratio = 0.10
        navy_ratio = 0
        states = {
          110
          916
          236
        }
        states_filter = {
          wet_ult_revolt_site = yes
        }
        keep_all_characters = yes
        wet_ult_rebel_cleanup = yes
        set_country_flag = wet_ult_claimant
        H06 = {
          clear_array = wet_ult_rebel
          add_to_array = {
            wet_ult_rebel = PREV
          }
        }
        if = {
          limit = {
            H07 = {
              exists = no
            }
          }
          recruit_character = WET_H07_leader
          add_country_leader_role = {
            character = WET_H07_leader
            promote_leader = yes
            country_leader = {
              ideology = wet_unaligned_cadai
              expire = "2902.1.31.1"
              traits = {
                wet_ruler_h07_leader
              }
            }
          }
# WET ruler command: appointment
hidden_effect = { WET_H07_leader = { wet_ruler_enable_field_marshal = yes } }

        }

# WET ruler command: civil-war successor
hidden_effect = { wet_ruler_current_command = yes }
      }
    }
    else = {
      start_civil_war = {
        ideology = neutrality
        ruling_party = neutrality
        size = 0.2
        army_ratio = 0.2
        air_ratio = 0.10
        navy_ratio = 0
        states = {
          110
          916
          236
        }
        states_filter = {
          wet_ult_revolt_site = yes
        }
        keep_all_characters = yes
        wet_ult_rebel_cleanup = yes
        set_country_flag = wet_ult_claimant

          clear_array = wet_ult_rebel
          add_to_array = {
            wet_ult_rebel = PREV
          }
        }
        if = {
          limit = {
            H07 = {
              exists = no
            }
          }
          recruit_character = WET_H07_leader
          add_country_leader_role = {
            character = WET_H07_leader
            promote_leader = yes
            country_leader = {
              ideology = wet_unaligned_cadai
              expire = "2902.1.31.1"
              traits = {
                wet_ruler_h07_leader
              }
            }
          }
# WET ruler command: appointment
hidden_effect = { WET_H07_leader = { wet_ruler_enable_field_marshal = yes } }

        }

# WET ruler command: civil-war successor
hidden_effect = { wet_ruler_current_command = yes }
      }
    }
    add_ideas = wet_ult_schism_cohesion
    country_event = {

    }

      clr_state_flag = wet_ult_prison

    clear_array = wet_ult_prison

wet_ult_capture_aliathra = {
  if = {
    limit = {
      tag = A13
      exists = yes
      has_country_flag = wet_ult_capture_operation_succeeded
      NOT = {
        has_global_flag = wet_story_aliathra_captured
      }
      NOT = {
        has_global_flag = wet_story_aliathra_dead
      }
      capital_scope = {
        is_fully_controlled_by = A13
      }
      H06 = {
        exists = yes
        has_character = WET_ULT_Aliathra
      }
    }
    clr_country_flag = wet_ult_capture_operation_succeeded
    set_global_flag = wet_story_aliathra_captured
    set_global_flag = wet_ult_aliathra_captive
# WET ruler command: captivity set_global_flag
hidden_effect = { every_country = { limit = { has_character = WET_ULT_Aliathra } WET_ULT_Aliathra = { wet_ruler_suspend_command = yes } } }

    set_country_flag = wet_ult_custodian
    H06 = {
      add_to_variable = {
        wet_ult_custody_generation = 1
      }

wet_ult_refresh_custody = {
  if = {
    limit = {
      has_global_flag = wet_ult_aliathra_captive
    }
    for_each_scope_loop = {
      array = wet_ult_prison
      if = {
        limit = {
          controller = {
            NOT = {
              has_country_flag = wet_ult_custodian
            }
          }
        }
        controller = {
          if = {
            limit = {
              OR = {
                tag = H06
                is_in_faction_with = H06
                is_subject_of = H06
              }
            }
            H06 = {
              set_global_flag = wet_ult_rescue_by_force
              wet_ult_release_aliathra = yes
            }
          }
          else = {
            every_country = {
              limit = {
                has_country_flag = wet_ult_custodian
              }
              clr_country_flag = wet_ult_custodian
            }
            set_country_flag = wet_ult_custodian
            H06 = {
              clear_array = wet_ult_custodian
              add_to_array = {
                wet_ult_custodian = PREV
              }
              add_to_variable = {
                wet_ult_custody_generation = 1
              }
              clr_country_flag = wet_ult_prison_located
            }
          }
        }
      }
    }
  }
}
wet_ult_sacrifice_aliathra = {
  H06 = {
    wet_ult_refresh_custody = yes
  }
  if = {
    limit = {
      has_country_flag = wet_ult_custodian
      check_variable = {
        wet_ult_custody_token = H06.wet_ult_custody_generation
      }
      H06 = {
        wet_ult_captive = yes
      }
      any_controlled_state = {
        has_state_flag = wet_ult_prison
      }
    }
    H06 = {
      for_each_scope_loop = {
        array = wet_ult_custodian
        clr_country_flag = wet_ult_custodian
      }
      for_each_scope_loop = {
        array = wet_ult_prison
        clr_state_flag = wet_ult_prison
      }
      clear_array = wet_ult_custodian
      clear_array = wet_ult_prison
      clr_global_flag = wet_ult_aliathra_captive
# WET ruler command: captivity clr_global_flag
hidden_effect = { every_country = { limit = { has_character = WET_ULT_Aliathra } WET_ULT_Aliathra = { wet_ruler_restore_command = yes } } }

      clr_country_flag = wet_ult_prison_located
      add_to_variable = {
        wet_ult_custody_generation = 1
      }
      set_global_flag = wet_story_aliathra_dead
      retire_character = WET_ULT_Aliathra
      country_event = {
        id = wet_ulthuan.22
      }
    }
  }
}

wet_ult_tick_recipient = {
  if = {
    limit = {
      check_variable = {
        wet_ult_offer_amends_cooldown
        >
        0
      }
    }
    subtract_from_variable = {
      wet_ult_offer_amends_cooldown = 1
    }
  }
  if = {
    limit = {
      has_country_flag = wet_ult_offer_amends
    }
    subtract_from_variable = {
      wet_ult_offer_amends_days = 1
    }
    if = {
      limit = {
        OR = {
          check_variable = {
            wet_ult_offer_amends_days
            <
            1
          }
          NOT = {
            AND = {
              wet_ult_unappeased_dwarf = yes
              H06 = {
                is_subject = no
                has_political_power
                >
                74
                has_equipment = {
                  infantry_equipment
                  >
                  499
                }
              }
            }
          }
        }
      }
      if = {
        limit = {
          has_country_flag = wet_ult_offer_amends
        }
        clr_country_flag = wet_ult_offer_amends
        set_variable = {
          wet_ult_offer_amends_days = 0
        }
        set_variable = {
          wet_ult_offer_amends_cooldown = 180
        }
      }
    }
  }
  if = {
    limit = {
      check_variable = {
        wet_ult_offer_empire_cooldown
        >
        0
      }
    }
    subtract_from_variable = {
      wet_ult_offer_empire_cooldown = 1
    }
  }
  if = {
    limit = {
      has_country_flag = wet_ult_offer_empire
    }
    subtract_from_variable = {
      wet_ult_offer_empire_days = 1
    }
    if = {
      limit = {
        OR = {
          check_variable = {
            wet_ult_offer_empire_days
            <
            1
          }
          NOT = {
            AND = {
              exists = yes
              is_faction_leader = yes
              is_in_faction_with = A01
              has_government = democratic
              H06 = {
                wet_ult_empire_ready = yes
                NOT = {
                  any_enemy_country = {
                    is_in_faction_with = PREV.PREV
                  }
                }
              }
            }
          }
        }
      }
      if = {
        limit = {
          has_country_flag = wet_ult_offer_empire
        }
        clr_country_flag = wet_ult_offer_empire
        set_variable = {
          wet_ult_offer_empire_days = 0
        }
        set_variable = {
          wet_ult_offer_empire_cooldown = 180
        }
      }
    }
  }
  if = {
    limit = {
      check_variable = {
        wet_ult_offer_pact_B01_cooldown
        >
        0
      }
    }
    subtract_from_variable = {
      wet_ult_offer_pact_B01_cooldown = 1
    }
  }
  if = {
    limit = {
      has_country_flag = wet_ult_offer_pact_B01
    }
    subtract_from_variable = {
      wet_ult_offer_pact_B01_days = 1
    }
    if = {
      limit = {
        OR = {
          check_variable = {
            wet_ult_offer_pact_B01_days
            <
            1
          }
          NOT = {
            AND = {
              tag = B01
              exists = yes
              is_subject = no
              NOT = {
                has_war_with = H06
              }
              H06 = {
                wet_ult_sovereign = yes
              }
            }
          }
        }
      }

              always = yes
            }

              }

                wet_ult_offer_amends_cooldown
                <
                1
              }

                has_country_flag = wet_ult_offer_amends_accepted
              }
              OR = {
                NOT = {
                  has_country_flag = wet_ult_offer_amends_attempted
                }
                H06 = {
                  always = yes
                }
              }
            }
          }

          }
        }
      }
    }
    wet_ult_reconcile_benefits = yes
  }
